**Current edition** 25 November 2026, evening, Asia/Jakarta  
**Current state** Editable UISP16 proposal draft delivered. Survey Kit v3 is ready for setup. Survey publication, response collection, and submission readiness have yet to be verified.

This opening update takes precedence over the dated material below. The full earlier 25 November planning pack and its nested 23 November archive are preserved byte for byte. ## Start here

- Use **LihatDulu_UISP16_Proposal.docx** as the current editable working draft and **LihatDulu_UISP16_Proposal_Preview.pdf** as the reviewed export. The earlier uploaded Word file was the layout authority for building this draft.
- Use **LihatDulu-Survey-Kit-v3.zip** for future survey setup. It is open to students and non-students. Students are expected to make up much of the response pool, but their actual share will come from the data. The proposal's lead market remains mahasiswa kos and young renters in Jabodetabek.
- Collect the survey responses and exploratory interviews before adding primary findings to the proposal. Keep findings, observed prototype behavior, commercial assumptions, and future capabilities tied to their own evidence. Finance and team authorship are outstanding.
- Use [Proposal Workflow](https://chatgpt.com/skills?skill_id=6b06b7f51e7c8191ac3af63e3113986d) and the supplied Word structure for subsequent proposal work. The prior planning pack below contains the full commercial argument, funding sequence, operations, figures, appendix requirements and three-pass review plan.

## Proposal delivered and verified

The editable draft follows the supplied cover, header/footer artwork, styles, two A4 sections, and five-chapter structure. It has 19 body pages including the Executive Summary, with seven appendices: A business model, B research methods and interview space, C prototype and R&D, D market and competitors, E finance, F operations and quality control, and G marketing. The main proposal is the finished service design in Indonesian through checkout, handover and repeat use. The document preserves example versus measured evidence boundaries. Chapter headings use periods, and the body and figure captions underwent a visible punctuation check.

- **Business** serves a broad household furniture catalogue in Jabodetabek, with mahasiswa kos and young renters as the lead marketing segment. Keep free self-serve listings with photos, seller-supplied dimensions, and a condition description. Show 3D when useful. Use pickup or optional third-party delivery. The proposed earnings are a flat buyer service fee and a percentage seller commission when an order is completed, with rates open. Proposed business catalogue embeds and an API tier come later. Retain the founder or non-dilutive funding, pre-seed, and seed sequencing below.
- **Market** uses an estimated annualized national household meubelair spending benchmark of **Rp8.71 trillion on a 2024 basis**. The calculation is Rp2,577 per person per month × 281,603,800 people × 12, using BPS Susenas March 2024, Table 2.3, and Statistik Indonesia 2025, Table 3.1.2. The serviceable Jabodetabek resale share still needs evidence for a numerical SAM. The annual year-three GMV target is **1% of SAM**, tested against implied orders, listings, sell-through and handover capacity. Appendix D's per-Rp100-billion worked case illustrates scale with hypothetical item values. It is not a SAM or revenue projection.
- **Product and R&D** include real captures of the prepared Sora seller and buyer views, with room positioning, plus the separately verified chair comparison. The public demo fixture for the real chair records 101 source frames, 105,463 ms to ready, 1,950,948 bytes (about 1.95 MB) for the refined GLB, and 19,246 triangles both before and after refinement. Missing geometry is visible and photos lead. The earlier same-chair job reported removal of 354 fragment triangles. The draft charts the documented counts and uses the matched views from the verified public fixture. The archive's ambiguous before/after photo was not used as visual proof of fragment removal. Appendix C also documents the separate seven-clip local series and the rejected silhouette completion approach. Guided capture in the current recorder is timed. Adaptive coverage and missing-angle feedback are the intended design.
- **Review** completed three stages on the delivered draft: judge and commercial argument, sources/calculations/R&D traceability, and rendered Word/PDF navigation. Nine XeLaTeX diagrams compiled and 12 figures were embedded. Word XML contains 87 resolvable internal links, and the delivered PDF has 38 TOC links and 108 total links. One appendix TOC link was restored in the PDF after conversion. The BMC fits on one page. The DOCX specifies Times New Roman. Local preview and figure generation used Liberation Serif. Verify the final submission export in the intended typeface, after all content has been integrated.
- **Submission gaps** are Bab V, Lampiran E, financial results in the summary, §1.3, interview-derived findings or persona evidence, and cover and authorship fields. The 25-body-page cap is from the project's organizer format notes. The [public organizer page](https://www.uispreneur.com/competition) lists the semifinal proposal deadline as 27 November 2026. It does not show the detailed page-count rule. Recheck the exact timetable, final DOCX and PDF flow, contents and links once the teammate and field inputs arrive.

## Survey decisions and exact protocol

The first kit's listing and 3D stages largely repeated cabinet views. Feedback asked to make the physical imperfections legible. The user approved prioritizing condition evidence and using the existing Sora example without a new physical cabinet. The revision preserves the original baseline and adds the details the buyer can act on. The form labels the imperfections as **example condition images**. The sample cabinet is not presented as proof of a physical inspection.

| Stage in v3 | What it does |
|---|---|
| Welcome and profile | Title **Cara memilih furnitur bekas secara online**. About four minutes, voluntary, with no name or email collection. The optional **Apakah saat ini kamu berstatus mahasiswa?** item has Ya and Tidak options and does not affect routing. |
| Search history | Required question about the last 12 months. People who searched and considered at least one ad answer the actual experience items. People who searched without considering a specific listing and people who did not search go straight to the Sora example. |
| Actual experience | Anchor the information-gap and behavior questions to the last specific listing considered. Ask what finally happened, including purchasing that item, buying another, not buying, or still considering. |
| Baseline | Show the two exact images embedded in the original kit, front and open cabinet. The kit names source build `8b404df`. Use the same Sora scenario across both stages: secondhand example, Rp2.450.000, Jakarta Selatan, 90 × 47,5 × 120 cm closed. |
| Condition reveal | Display the three-condition overview and the scratch close-up, actual screenshots from release `29961f0`. The details are an 11 cm scratch at the left door, an approximately 8 × 5 mm chipped upper-right corner, and a 5.5 cm ring mark at the top. Use `/barang/sora?tab=photos`, Catatan Kondisi, then Goresan pintu kiri to recapture. |
| Matched measures | Before and after the reveal, ask the same confidence question on a five-point scale, the same next-action item, and the same secondary purchase-intent item with a separate “Belum bisa menilai” option. End with optional open feedback on what influenced their action and what information is missing. The live generator ends there. There is no separate 3D or room stage. |

**Setup and status.** Copy the entire `LihatDulu-Survey.gs` from v3 into a new Apps Script project and run `createLihatDuluSurvey` once. It creates an unpublished Google Form and a linked response sheet. It logs the edit URL, responder URL and sheet URL. The two baseline JPEGs and two condition PNGs are embedded with their actual MIME types. The final ZIP, JavaScript syntax, all three routes, same question order and image bytes passed local checks with a mocked Forms API. Actual creation, phone preview, anonymous responder access and publication require the owner. At the v2 design discussion the user explicitly said the earlier survey had collected zero responses. No later response count, form URL or confirmation of v3 publication was supplied. The generator does not change an existing public form. If collection is already underway on another version, document the exact form seen by each response batch before pooling them.

**Analysis.** Population of interest for recruitment is broad. Students may dominate the realized sample. Report student, non-student and missing-status counts as observed. Use only those with a specific recent listing for real-world barriers and outcomes. Report the applicable denominator, multiple-answer counts and any exclusions for contradictory “Tidak ada” responses. For demo changes, use respondents with both answers. Match the first repeated field with baseline and the second with the condition reveal. Report confidence up, unchanged and down with paired N, and describe next actions separately. A more confident decision to skip can be a useful outcome. The fixed-order demo measures self-reported changes after viewing more detail and does not isolate a causal effect, actual conversion or an incremental 3D effect. Describe the realized sample and avoid claiming it represents all furniture shoppers or Jabodetabek. Keep the planned interviews, at least three buyers and one seller, as a separate evidence stream. Add only consented, anonymized primary material.

## Outreach message

The user wants short informal Indonesian copy without the LihatDulu brand name. Advertise the same **Pitch Deck Juara 1 MCC Billions** free to every completed response. Do not promise immediate automated delivery or imply a specific mechanism that has not been configured. The generator includes a basic thank-you message and does not distribute the deck. The organizer/user will share it. Use the actual responder URL once the form is published.

# LihatDulu Master AI Context Pack

**Current edition:** 25 November 2026, Asia/Jakarta  
**Status:** Proposal production plan complete. Drafting and editing the Word proposal await the user's explicit go-ahead.  
**Word base:** [LihatDulu_UISP16_Proposal (1).docx](https://chatgpt.com/library?file_id=libfile_5b67a2c0fe608191bfb654ba79b1c9a9&account_id=personal)  
**Working references:** [Funding roadmap](https://chatgpt.com/library?file_id=libfile_a0e2230a60d88191863978977f14b4ab), [Proposal Workflow](https://chatgpt.com/skills?skill_id=6b06b7f51e7c8191ac3af63e3113986d), [BPC Studio](https://github.com/Fafauzif/BPC-Studio/tree/b0f6a9226b60896dffe3795578a09ffc20299a37)

The latest user decisions and this production plan govern the next turn. The full 23 November edition follows as a byte-for-byte archive. Its references and research history still matter. Its undecided business choices, earlier skill link, and release assumptions are historical. Use the actual current organizer rules and artifacts for compliance and factual claims.

## Decisions to carry into production

| Decision | Working brief |
|---|---|
| Venture and geography | LihatDulu is a marketplace for secondhand household furniture in Jabodetabek. The catalogue accepts seating, tables, storage, beds, and other household furniture from the start. Lead the customer story with mahasiswa kos and young renters furnishing a room or moving. Individual sellers and recurring secondhand sellers supply inventory. |
| Core proposition | Sellers use their own phones to document furniture. Buyers use original condition evidence, listed measurements, interactive 3D when usable, and a room-placement preview where supported. Explain the whole service from listing through handover and repeat use in the present tense as designed. |
| Listing and capture | Standard listing and self-serve capture are free. Original photos, measurements supplied by the seller, and a condition description are enough to publish. An eligible 3D model can appear later. Adaptive prompts to fill missing angles and aid framing are the intended design. The release contains time-based on-screen directions. The public chair exhibit provides evidence from one real item. |
| Fulfillment | Buyer pickup or optional third-party delivery. Seller retains the furniture until handover. The platform maintains the order and listing evidence and provides a route to report a discrepancy. Describe this as the operating design. Use partner categories until actual partnerships can be verified. |
| Revenue | Both sides contribute on a completed order. Working mechanism: a small flat buyer service fee, disclosed in the price breakdown, and a percentage seller commission on item value, shown in net proceeds and deducted at settlement. Track delivery separately. Confirm the definition of completion and treatment of refunds in the eventual model. Paid documentation and mandatory listing fees were dropped. Rates and financial outputs remain open. |
| B2B and funding | Marketplace first. Founder capital and potential non-dilutive funding support the initial operation, pre-seed supports automation and introduction of paid catalogue embeds when measurement and support capacity justify it, seed supports expansion and recurring B2B growth. Planned B2B offer is a hosted 3D catalogue subscription with a usage allowance, then an API tier. Use the three stages in the operating roadmap as conditional milestones. Funding sources, amounts, cash forecasts and financing decisions stay for the finance work. |
| Market sizing | Top-down. TAM is annual Indonesian household furniture spending as the sector benchmark. SAM is annual spend in secondhand household furniture within serviceable Jabodetabek, adjusted transparently for category and geographic coverage where relevant. SOM is an aspirational Year 3 annual GMV target of 1% of SAM, tested against active listings, plausible sell-through, item value, and handover capacity. Show reachable capacity and in-app capture separately. Keep GMV, recognized fees, collection and company revenue unambiguous. |
| Proposal scope | The attached five-chapter Word scaffold controls. Update the BMC to reflect this proposal. The preliminary BMC does not bind the team. The user confirmed the target segment, photo-first listing rule, pickup plus optional delivery, broad categories, and Year 3 target. This planning round closes clarification. |
| Reserved inputs | Leave Bab V and Lampiran E blank beneath their structure on the first authorized draft. Leave financial outputs out of the Executive Summary. Reserve §1.3, interview-derived persona evidence in §4.1, and findings in Lampiran B. Proposed customer segments can still be described and supported with available secondary sources. The previously prepared interview instruments can be indexed or attached as blank methods material. |

## Final DOCX production plan

### Template and page budget

Use the attached DOCX as the master and work from a copy when authorized. Baseline SHA-256 is 9c3e94a1f0f4a759d932b67812986aa88c9b8305c01feea1441e6c996ff5ac93. Package inspection found two A4 sections with 2.54 cm margins, a live TOC field, 41 bookmark starts and 41 internal hyperlink elements before editing, and two media parts. Preserve the cover, section and page-number setup, header/footer artwork, styles, heading hierarchy, and front matter sequence. Use 14 pt bold chapter headings, 12 pt bold subheadings, Times New Roman 12 pt body, approved justified 1.5 spacing and first-line indent. Use the punctuation pattern **BAB V. Financial Strategy** and **Lampiran A. Business Model Canvas**. Remove em dashes throughout visible content and regenerated fields, avoid semicolons in prose, and keep formal English chapter names, established framework names, and natural Indonesian proposal prose. Fill cover and authorship details only from supplied team information.

The supplied format notes record a 25-page body maximum excluding the cover, contents, Statement of Authorship, references and appendices. Use 19 body pages as the working draft and 20 if placed exhibits justify the extra page. Verify the actual rendered count from Executive Summary through the last counted page. Preserve space for teammate inputs within the eventual cap. The organizer's public page lists the 27 November 2026 semifinal proposal deadline. Use the actual committee guide and portal for submission rules and exact cutoff. [Organizer competition timeline](https://www.uispreneur.com/competition)

| Section | Working pages | Narrative and exhibits |
|---|---:|---|
| Executive Summary | 1 | Write after the body. Define the buying decision, seller offer, business mechanism, opening market, strongest verified evidence, and expansion logic. Integrate finance and interview outcomes when supplied. |
| Bab I. Problem Background & Validation | 3 | §1.1 describes how people buy and sell furniture secondhand. §1.2 narrows to size, condition, and handover uncertainty and the work sellers do to answer it. Use secondary sources and dated observations for these claims. §1.3 holds exploratory buyer and seller results for team input. |
| Bab II. Business Model | 6 | §2.1 defines the venture and the two customer groups. §2.2 maps the buyer's and seller's value. §2.3 follows one Sora listing from seller capture to buyer inspection, room preview, checkout and handover as the designed service. §2.4 shows the actual chair evidence and the R&D decisions. §2.5 gives both fee events and later B2B subscriptions. |
| Bab III. Operation Strategy | 4 | §§3.1–3.3 show operating responsibilities, payment and fulfillment handoffs, capture and QC, and the capacity constraint that determines output. §§3.4–3.6 show the Gantt, milestones tied to the three funding stages, KPI ownership and responses to the main risks. |
| Bab IV. Marketing Strategy | 5 | §4.1 defines the initial audience and positioning. Hold interview-based persona conclusions. §4.2 shows the market layers, 1% target and throughput check. §4.3 compares credible alternatives and connects SWOT to decisions. §§4.4–4.6 show the 4P offer and channel sequence, conversion through completed handover, and why buyers return and sellers publish again. |
| Bab V. Financial Strategy | Reserved | Keep §5.1, §5.2 and Lampiran E in the outline. Reserve fees, projections, costs, profitability, cash flow, funding, break-even and investment metrics, including NPV, IRR and Payback Period where used. Remove scaffold prompts from draft prose and leave the reserved content empty. |

The two priority exhibits are the Sora customer journey and the R&D figure. Keep the main body readable with the appendices closed. Prefer a strong image pair or shallow chart to several low-information graphics.

### R&D exhibit and source controls

- Lead with the real chair public exhibit. The release fixture reports 101 source frames, about 1 min 45 sec to ready, a 1,950,948-byte refined GLB (about 1.95 MB), and 19,246 triangles. Eight original stills ship with the saved public fixture. The raw and refined geometry share a 19,246 triangle count. Backrest and base defects remain visible. A projection hole-share measurement near 18.8% is diagnostic, includes possible physical openings, and is suited to the appendix with a definition. It is not an accuracy score.
- Use separate panels and captions for the earlier masked Photo Scan job, the latest public chair job, and the separate seven local captures of the same chair. The earlier refinement log records 22,123 to 21,769 triangles, with 354 removed. Its archived comparison image appears to show a fragment in both rows. Rerender from the matching raw/refined meshes and verify the visible finding before claiming picture-based proof. Use verifiable records in a table if the matching visual cannot be regenerated.
- Use the exact saved artifact and matched framing, crop and scale when comparing both mesh stages. The frame-to-mesh angles may be approximate and should be labeled as such where applicable. Attach source and render locally before placing the figure. Prefer cropped actual captures, with diagram labels and restrained arrows. Draw adaptive arrows or missing-angle cues as a labeled design illustration if that behavior has not been implemented or verified.
- An appendix comparison can show why photo-guided geometry completion was rejected, if the exact record can be verified. Keep different job timing scopes, provider settings, hardware and outputs visible. The available detail notes and public fixture disagree on refinement substage timing, so use the agreed total for the public caption and reconcile per-stage timing before plotting it. No new live scan is needed for this draft.
- The prepared Sora cabinet shows the product experience. The real chair supports reconstruction, refinement and photo fallback for one physical object. Claims about category-wide accuracy, automatically measured size, or completed live purchases require their own evidence. Attribute provider reconstruction to KIRI and the rest to the relevant LihatDulu components.

[Public chair job fixture](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/blob/29961f0/public/demo/kursi/job.json) · [Raw/refined research notes](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/blob/29961f0/docs/handoff/refinement.md) · [Capture implementation](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/blob/29961f0/src/scan-recorder.js)

Pelaksanaan Pemasaran** | G.1 channel and acquisition matrix, G.2 funnel stage definitions and conversion/retention measurement, G.3 example campaign or message flow if it illustrates the actual buyer or seller acquisition mechanism. Link interview-supported persona evidence to B when supplied. |

### LaTeX, TikZ and Word integration

- LaTeX is the figure production format. Use TikZ for the technical pipeline, topology, swimlanes, compact Gantt and market layers. Use PGFPlots for defensible quantitative comparisons. Use TikZ with the original screenshots for multi-panel alignment and annotations. Keep exact-data tables, chapter headings, paragraphs, captions, links and BMC cells editable in native Word where practical.
- For each authored diagram or plot, keep the source data or diagram specification, standalone TEX, referenced image assets, vector PDF and a verified Word placement asset. Use a Word-compatible vector asset when it survives the render path, or a 300 dpi PNG at final physical size. Keep the compiler result and source register private. Figure dimensions and file weights should follow the actual book, with wide, shallow exhibits near 15–15.9 cm in the body when appropriate. Aim for roughly 9–10 pt placed labels or any larger verified organizer minimum. Reflow dense material before shrinking text.
- Use black Times New Roman document text, a restrained green and neutral figure palette, restrained accents only for distinctions, and descriptive Indonesian captions in Word. Keep actual screenshot appearance and meaningful image contrast. Use a common scale and baseline where the chart compares values. Label schematic boundaries and define period, geography, units and denominator. Keep editable sources for regeneration alongside the project.
- Use a real vector TeX compile for final analytical figures. Check fonts, missing glyphs and overfull boxes. Use the fallback renderer only to continue layout work if TeX is unavailable. Record the actual renderer and any font substitution in the handoff, then compile with a working compatible environment for the requested final figures. Keep the competition PDF under the recorded 30 MB limit if requested for submission QA.

### Reference use and source research

Read examples for the specific exhibit decision. ReFeel supplies the two-sided buyer decision and traceable market sizing, especially physical pp. 7–8, 31 and 40. Dua supplies a fair comparison and a Gantt tied to ownership and indicators, especially physical pp. 48 and 58. Use JejakBalik for the relationship between a short body and an inspectable dossier, and ReGenCity or the Ruang Rapi example for native Word tables and appendix navigation. Urfalogy printed p. 3 anchors the pairing of numerical results and actual visual output. Create original LihatDulu material and cite evidence for this venture separately.

For actual drafting, match sources to each claim. Start market verification with the relevant [BPS household expenditure publication](https://www.bps.go.id/id/publication/2026/10/05/4e167126f0dc9e67c8e602b2/pengeluaran-untuk-konsumsi-penduduk-indonesia-maret-2026.html), then validate furniture scope, reference period, geographic weighting and any independent secondhand share evidence. If a SAM input remains unsupported, keep an explicitly labeled scenario or reserve the value. Check open-access research on online secondhand purchase uncertainty and the evidence buyers use. Compare alternatives with current, traceable product observations. List open assumptions internally and cite verifiable primary publications in APA where suited to the brief. [BPC Studio atlas](https://github.com/Fafauzif/BPC-Studio/blob/b0f6a9226b60896dffe3795578a09ffc20299a37/docs/reference-atlas.md) · [BPC Studio chart gallery](https://github.com/Fafauzif/BPC-Studio/blob/b0f6a9226b60896dffe3795578a09ffc20299a37/docs/chart-gallery.md)

### Three review and improvement passes after authorization

1. **Judge and commercial argument.** Read the exact draft body with the appendices closed. Check the required headings and official criteria if available, opening segment, why the buyer chooses it, both fee events, who owns each handover, repeat use and the logic from funding stages to scale. Revise the argument and Indonesian prose. Keep present-tense descriptions of the adopted design and precise attribution of observed results.
2. **Sources, arithmetic and engineering.** Map substantive claims to original evidence or labeled assumptions. Recompute market and order arithmetic. Reconcile buyer price, seller proceeds, platform revenue and GMV. Verify source scope, partner status, interview consent, actual vs designed capture, exact R&D versions, and figure labels against the underlying values. Render from canonical inputs and check the exported charts at their actual insertion size. Record any pending finance or interviews as planned gaps outside the proposal prose.
3. **Exact Word and PDF output.** Preserve the template sections and artwork. Update real Word navigation fields, section numbering, appendix bookmarks and links. Open the rendered Word/PDF page by page for pagination, captions, margins, fonts, figure size, table repeats and empty gaps. Check cover and header/footer against the input. Scan human-facing XML, metadata fields as relevant and extracted PDF text for em dashes, placeholder directions, broken links, comments and tracked changes. Verify each body-to-exhibit and return jump, and test any QR or URL in a signed-out browser. Re-render and recheck affected pages after repairs. Use a private pass, revise, pending or N/A record for fulfillment, accuracy, completeness, readability and finish. Apply the workflow's judge scoring during the relevant full review. Evidence gaps block claims regardless of score.

## Real scan evidence and remaining limits

- **Earlier masked Photo Scan Ultra.** The same Golden chair clip supplied 101 stills. The published result page reports **3 min 42 sec**, **2.3 MB**, and **21,769 triangles**. The older featureless mesh took **39 min 56 sec** total and was ~85 MiB. The masked result isolates the chair better, with visible loss of the mesh backrest and distorted base. Keep the 3 min 42 sec exhibit separate from the newer live refinement job. [Result screenshot](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/blob/5aaf225/docs/handoff/assets/kiri-chair-result.png) · [Four poses](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/blob/5aaf225/docs/handoff/assets/kiri-chair-poses.png)
- **New live refinement run, job `a0630d0e`.** The same chair supplied 101 stills again. Recorded time to ready was **1 min 45 sec**, with **302 ms** in local refinement. Raw GLB was 1,932,024 bytes, refined GLB 1,950,948 bytes, and both contained 19,246 triangles. The source-versus-model comparison still shows a large backrest gap, missing seat surface, and warped or disconnected lower geometry. The projected-hole metric reached **18.76% on the Y projection** on both versions. That figure measures empty depth columns in that projection and includes intentional openings. Use the source photos to distinguish actual openings from missing reconstructed surfaces. [Photos versus refined model](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/blob/0667dcd/docs/handoff/assets/refine-source-vs-model.jpg) · [Viewer with refinement](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/blob/0667dcd/docs/handoff/assets/refine-live-result.jpg)
- **What shipped.** A shared CPU-side GLB stage removes eligible small fragments, welds seams, recalculates normals, fills limited micro-cracks, and frames the mesh. On an earlier chair artifact the cleanup removed a 354-triangle shard. The original GLB remains available. The viewer at `/pindai/:id` exposes the refined result, original scan and original stills, with an approximation label. Local retry starts from the saved GLB. A failed refinement can fall back to raw. A visually poor but successfully processed mesh can still lead the viewer. Making photos the default for unusable geometry was proposed and remains pending.
- **What was tested and rejected.** Photo-silhouette visual-hull completion produced a slab across openings. The run notes report silhouette IoU **0.026** versus **0.144** for the unrepaired baseline on that experiment. Large-gap or major-part reconstruction remains unresolved. The only real physical item that produced a usable test artifact was the chair. Two synthetic orbit submissions failed, and the real cabinet upload also failed upstream using 24 images derived from eight distinct views. A different product through the same frozen pipeline without item-specific edits remains untested. [Refinement report and experiment ledger](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/blob/0667dcd/docs/handoff/refinement.md)
- **Verification and credit status.** The commit reports **308 unit passes**, **173 KIRI test passes**, **31/31 KIRI verification**, **26/26 V8 verification**, and a green build. The evening repo check verified the refs and inspected the published comparison images. It did not rerun those tests or submit a scan. The user offered a new key with ten credits. The documented ledger ends at seven, while the commit and session summary say six remained. Treat that balance as unverified. A future authorized run should check the live balance first. Keys belong in the private server environment.

## UISP16 presentation and handoff priorities

1. **Lead with Sora.** Use the prepared cabinet to show how a buyer inspects condition, checks listed dimensions, and previews room placement. Walk through the exact judging URL on the intended device. Keep a local demo backup.
2. **Use the real chair in §2.4 Proof of Concept.** Pair an original frame with a viewer image from the same run. The latest run is the exhibit for the saved GLB, automated cleanup, and original-photo workflow. State the remaining geometry defects in the caption or paragraph and link the full comparisons in Lampiran C. Preserve the separate provenance of the prepared cabinet. Any run metrics in the figure must match that exact artifact.
3. **Prioritize customer evidence.** Use the existing forms with at least three buyers and one seller, then bring documented examples into §1.3 and the personas into §4.1. The approved DOCX remains a five-chapter scaffold with 12 pt bold subheadings. No interview findings, payer decision, funding selection, or verified judge-facing release were recorded in this update.
4. **If scan R&D resumes later**, start with one real matte cabinet and a dense continuous orbit. Run the existing refinement settings unchanged to test transfer. Use the same capture and evaluation workflow on further product types as evidence allows. Reserve professional-grade automatic completion as a future objective. The chair-specific manual patch was discussed and superseded by the requirement for shared automatic refinement. No hand-built replacement was delivered.

## UISP16 format and decision history

The first discussion drew on Urfalogy for early product evidence. It explored the example TOC images for recognizable framework labels, marketing journey headings, and a more navigable business argument. The first six-chapter proposal used a 22-page working budget with six pages for product and proof, five for market and marketing, two for finance, and a separate execution chapter. After the organizer's **Struktur Paper** image arrived, the user approved moving the work into the five organizer chapters. The user explicitly removed **Target User & Customer Segment** as its own subchapter in Bab I. Its audience context now sits inside **1.2 Identifikasi Masalah Utama**. **Problem Validation** was renumbered to **1.3**. The formal STP and buyer/seller personas remain together in **4.1**.

The required front sequence is **Cover → Daftar Isi → Statement of Authorship → Executive Summary**. The five organizer headings remain in English. The editable prompts and future proposal prose are in Bahasa Indonesia, with established labels such as STP, SWOT, Marketing Mix, Proof of Concept, KPIs, and Payback Period allowed in English. The user wants the middle ground on frameworks. Make required frameworks visible and compact, then use them to explain a decision or operating mechanism. The organizer includes SWOT, which now belongs alongside the competitor comparison. The BMC is the first appendix, immediately after the proposed references section and appendix heading. Referensi placement was an editorial choice because the Struktur Paper image gives no reference placement.

**Formatting authority:** use the corrected DOCX's approved cover, margins, header/footer artwork, page-number setup, spacing and chapter styling. The supplied corrected file has A4 portrait pages and 2.54 cm margins. The latest explicit instruction is **12 pt bold for subchapter headings**, including the applied Heading 2 style. Do not restore the 14 pt subchapter guidance from the 22 November archive. In the accepted corrected file the existing chapter style is 14 pt bold. Preserve that style in follow-up edits unless the user or a newer organizer instruction asks for a change. The layout uses Times New Roman, black headings, 12 pt body, 1.5 line spacing, and 0 pt after body paragraphs. Keep the approved embedded artwork and normal flowing pagination. The rendered DOCX currently has five pages including the cover because it contains an outline and short prompts. The following 22-page budget is for the eventual content, within the previously recorded 25-page body limit. Per the earlier format sheet, cover, contents, authorship, references, and appendices are excluded. Final submission details from that sheet are PDF up to 30 MB and `BP_UISP16_[Nama Tim]_LihatDulu.pdf`. Team details, declaration text, and references are editable placeholders. Verify the current organizer rules again at submission.

The finished scaffold uses true Word Heading 1 and Heading 2 paragraphs, a refreshable TOC field with 31 cached linked entries, and 10 forward/return appendix links. The cover comparison was pixel-identical to the new template. The check confirmed all 22 numbered subchapters render at 12 pt bold and that headers, footers, artwork, and section properties match the supplied template. After drafting, refresh the whole TOC and check page numbers plus internal links in the exact Word/PDF used for submission.

## Approved proposal structure and prompts

Recommended body allocation is **Executive Summary 1 + Bab I 3 + Bab II 6 + Bab III 5 + Bab IV 5 + Bab V 2 = 22 pages**, with three pages of headroom under the earlier 25-page ceiling. These are working allocations, not per-chapter organizer limits. The document contains short directions for the writer. Use the sections below to reconstruct the full intent.

| Section | Content to develop |
|---|---|
| **Executive Summary** | In a compact case, identify the problem, initial buyer/seller and payer, finished offer, revenue event, strongest proof, and material financial outcome from the eventual model. Write this once the main logic is settled. |
| **BAB I — Problem Background & Validation** | Establish the actual problem, who experiences it, and what the interviews add. |
| 1.1 Latar Belakang Masalah | How people search for, buy, and sell secondhand furniture online and what listing information is available to them. Develop scoped supporting evidence. |
| 1.2 Identifikasi Masalah Utama | Work the target audiences into the problem. Explain where buyers, initially including mahasiswa kos, struggle to assess condition, dimensions and room fit. Explain which missing details sellers need to supply and what effort that creates. The buyer, seller, and eventual payer can be different people. |
| 1.3 Problem Validation | Present the exploratory interviews with at least one seller and three buyers, real examples from their experiences, and the implications for product design. Link to Lampiran B. |
| **BAB II — Business Model** | Show the complete offer in use, substantiate what has been built, then explain how the business earns. |
| 2.1 Deskripsi Bisnis LihatDulu | Define the marketplace, who uses it and what each side gets. |
| 2.2 Solusi dan Unique Value Proposition | Explain how the seller presents the item and the buyer uses condition evidence, dimensions, and room previews to decide. |
| 2.3 Alur Penjual dan Pembeli | Follow one prepared cabinet example from seller documentation to buyer inspection, seller-provided dimensions, and room placement. Use current screenshots and label this as the authored example when relevant. |
| 2.4 Proof of Concept LihatDulu | Show a real source chair and the reconstructed result in the viewer. Report observed processing and visual limits alongside the exhibit. Use KIRI's featureless mesh as the strongest new example and link the test history in Lampiran C. The original scaffold was written before the new audit and its short prompt can now be expanded using the KIRI evidence. |
| 2.5 Revenue Model | Identify the payer, paid offer, price basis and the event that earns revenue. Cross-link the BMC in Lampiran A and carry the same earned unit into Bab V. |
| **BAB III — Operation Strategy** | Trace how a listing becomes usable, who performs the work, and how operating capacity expands. |
| 3.1 Operating System & Value Chain | Seller onboarding → documentation → review → publication → buyer support. Assign ownership of handoffs. |
| 3.2 Resources, Tools & Supply Chain | Where furniture inventory comes from, which party holds it, and how pickup or delivery works. Identify processing tools, capacity drivers, and any third-party services. |
| 3.3 Quality Control & Capacity Planning | Publication standard, retake route, staffing and processing constraints. Count attempts and accepted outputs separately where scan effort matters. |
| 3.4 Implementation Roadmap (Gantt Chart) | Stages for readiness, seller onboarding, launch to buyers, and expansion. Give each milestone an owner, dependency, and completion condition. |
| 3.5 Key Performance Indicators (KPIs) | Indicators such as listings published, share meeting the evidence standard, and sellers publishing again. Set periods and targets once they are supportable. |
| 3.6 Risk Mitigation | Tie each relevant risk to a trigger, action, and owner. Think through scan quality, seller participation, processing capacity, and release reliability. |
| **BAB IV — Marketing Strategy** | Pick a reachable market and show how the two sides of the marketplace are acquired and return. |
| 4.1 Customer Analysis, STP & User Personas | Explain selection and positioning, then humanize it with one seller and one buyer card derived from the interviews. Bab I shows the underlying episodes. This subchapter turns them into a target decision. |
| 4.2 Market Size (TAM–SAM–SOM) | Define units, geography, period, filters, and realistic Year 1 reach based on distribution and capacity. Link the calculations in Lampiran D. |
| 4.3 Competitor Analysis & SWOT | Compare marketplace listings, asking for extra detail and inspecting in person on buyer-relevant criteria. Use a compact SWOT to justify a positioning or launch decision. |
| 4.4 Go-to-Market Strategy & Marketing Mix (4P) | Explain the first supply of useful listings and how buyers discover them. Keep price analysis, offer, communication channels, and distribution together in a concise 4P table. Use the term Go-to-Market Strategy in place of the earlier Akuisisi Penjual dan Pembeli heading. |
| 4.5 Awareness, Consideration & Conversion | Walk through discovery, evaluation, and the conversion event that corresponds to the chosen business model. |
| 4.6 Retention & Repeat Use | Explain why buyers come back to inspect or revisit items and why sellers would list another item. A loyalty program is unnecessary for this purpose unless designed later. |
| **BAB V — Financial Strategy** | Two pages in the body with supporting calculations in Lampiran E. |
| 5.1 Revenue Projection, Cost Structure & Profitability | Project sales volume and revenue in the agreed unit, COGS or direct service costs, fixed/variable costs, profit and margin. Reconcile processing, repeats and labor with the operating chapter. |
| 5.2 Cash Flow, Funding & Investment Analysis | Show cash flow, use of capital, funding scheme, BEP, and NPV, IRR, and Payback Period if the team has the three calculations. Organizer allows a choice. The user expects their teammate may show all three. Keep the funding scheme open. |

**Appendices in order:** **A Business Model Canvas** (linked to 2.5), **B Wawancara Penjual dan Pembeli** (1.3), **C Dokumentasi Proof of Concept** (2.4), **D Analisis Pasar dan Pesaing** (4.2), **E Perhitungan Keuangan** (5.2). Each has a return link. Referensi keeps the APA placeholder and template hanging indent.

**Open commercial choices:** seller-paid enhanced listings for repeat resellers and a transaction commission were discussed as plausible revenue hypotheses. The user did not select one. A loan was mentioned as the finance teammate's possible route and the user explicitly asked to keep the proposal wording open. If a loan is later selected, specify amount/use, rate, term, installment schedule, and forecast cash coverage. Treat partner commitments, finance figures, market size, willingness to pay, and demand as unestablished until supported. If using KIRI in the financial model, distinguish observed run time, retry rates, and historical credits from a verified unit cost.

## Standardized buyer and seller interviews

**Purpose and recruitment.** Use the interviews primarily for problem validation, based on an actual secondhand-furniture buying or selling episode. Recruit at least three buyers, probably mahasiswa living in a kos, and at least one person who has actually sold secondhand furniture. Include buyers who searched or seriously considered an item and eventually walked away. The buyer form is deliberately identical for all buyer respondents. Copy it once per respondent and use codes such as P01/P02/P03 and J01. Each final form is a separately delivered four-page DOCX. Page 1 has the profile, consent and interviewer instructions, pages 2–3 the six core questions and optional product feedback, page 4 the documentation and synthesis fields. The layout includes ruled writing space, a separate direct-quote line for each core question, and an expandable photo or screenshot field. Both were checked as four-page documents with readiness, visual layout, and consent options on the render.

**Shared metadata fields.** Respondent code, date and duration, interviewer, method or general location. For buyers, collect student status, living context, product sought, and approximate timing. For sellers, record occasional or routine selling, product, channel, and approximate timing. Keep identifying details required for scheduling in a separate team record. Use respondent codes in the notes and proposal annex.

**Consent in the actual forms.** Read a short explanation before starting. Participation is voluntary and a respondent can skip questions or stop. Mark separate Yes/No choices for participating, recording audio, taking photos or screenshots for team documentation, including de-identified documentation in the proposal appendix, and using an anonymous quote. Record how the permissions were obtained and any limits. Obtain the relevant permission before capturing or reusing visual/audio material. Use a caption, date, respondent code, and file, transcript, or recording timestamp where applicable. Strip identifying details from chat/listing screenshots before attaching. Record direct quotes only when truly captured, and distinguish them from paraphrases. The team has committed to getting consent. Current artifacts contain blank fields and no respondent material.

**Interviewer method.** Use the same order and neutral framing in each role. Stay on one recent episode. Ask for specifics, what happened next, and what impact the issue had on the decision. Record success as well as trouble. Keep the product out of the core interview. Use examples only after hearing an unprompted answer. The optional concept question comes at the end after all core answers are recorded. Show the same short example and ask which information they would have used in the episode they just described and for what decision. This is secondary design feedback.

| Question | Buyer form asks | Seller form asks |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Recount the last time they looked for used furniture for the kos, including the item and the need behind it. | Recount the latest used-furniture sale, the item, how/where it was offered and what happened. |
| 2 | Identify the channels they tried and the reasons a particular item made the shortlist. | Explain photos and listing details prepared, and how the seller chose what to include. |
| 3 | Identify information the buyer could read directly and uncertainties remaining on the listing they considered most seriously. | Describe real buyer questions, recurring requests, and information first asked for after publishing. |
| 4 | Follow what they did next. If they contacted the seller, identify what they asked and how the response influenced the decision. | Describe how the seller answered and any time or work involved in providing extra evidence. |
| 5 | Explain how they checked whether the item would fit the kos and what they checked before deciding. Optional probes include dimensions, door access, delivery cost, and layout after the unprompted answer. | Trace one specific interaction that ended in a sale or fell through, from initial contact to decision. |
| 6 | Capture the outcome and main reason. For purchases, ask how the delivered item compared with the listing-based expectation. | Identify the most time-consuming or labor-intensive part between listing and handover, if any, and why. |

**End of each form.** Place the consented photo, call screenshot, or listing excerpt with its caption and evidence reference. Summarize what happened, what got in the way and what changed, how the respondent handled it, and the concrete quote, listing detail or timestamp that supports the summary. Use the exact reusable wording in the DOCX when interviewing. The table here preserves the intent in paraphrase.

**How to use the findings.** Put a concise account of recruitment and episodes in §1.3, anonymized support and method in Lampiran B, and two short buyer/seller personas in §4.1. Use the small sample as evidence of observed experience and specific decision barriers. Aggregate population claims, market share, and willingness to pay require appropriate additional evidence. Useful comparisons include observable wear, information requested, pickup/delivery constraints, physical fit, effort to prepare new evidence, and reasons someone chose a different solution. The forms are delivered. Recruitment, interviews, transcription, and analysis have yet to be documented in this chat.

## Figures and proof-of-concept narrative

Use LaTeX/TikZ for layout, diagrams, annotations, structured comparisons, and visual consistency. The primary items that require real inputs are screenshots, footage frames, and render output. They can still be composited in TikZ or laid out in Word. Use them to show what happened rather than generating approximations of the evidence.

| Placement | Proposed exhibit and input |
|---|---|
| §2.3 Alur Penjual dan Pembeli | Use three real LihatDulu screenshots for the same authored Sora cabinet. Follow the seller's documentation, the buyer finding a condition detail, and the buyer trying room placement. Add short action labels. This is the showcase for the designed end-to-end experience. |
| §2.4 Proof of Concept | Show an original still from the real chair footage beside the KIRI featureless-mesh result in LihatDulu. Pick comparable angles where available. The three-quarter opening pose shows the strongest recognizable chair. Include a second angle or nearby note showing the room texture bleed and weaker rear view. Caption the processing method, observed duration and file size. An original still and actual app capture are the evidence assets. |
| §1.3 Problem Validation, optional | Use a consented and anonymized listing screenshot only if it substantiates a real interview decision. Annotate one thing the buyer could see and one thing they needed to ask or check. |
| §4.1 User Personas | Two compact text cards are sufficient. Include the buyer/seller situation, the decision, and how they respond when information is absent. Anonymized, consented quotes can ground the cards. Stock portraits add little here. |
| §4.3 Competitor Analysis | Use a fair native table or a readable LaTeX table. An actual competitor screenshot can support a specific claim about placement of measurements or the request flow if needed. Put supporting captures in Lampiran D with a source locator. |
| Structured exhibits | Operating flow and value chain, Gantt, KPI and risk tables, TAM–SAM–SOM, SWOT, 4P, BMC, and financials are suitable for TikZ, LaTeX tables, or native Word tables. Choose the format based on reading size and editability. |

For proposal writing, lead the section with the finished buyer experience and use the real reconstructions as scoped technical evidence. The latest proposed proof story follows a simple sequence. Seven local camera originals demonstrated repeatable technical output from one chair. The local viewer fix improved centering and the best view. The cloud featureless mesh then recovered a visibly recognizable chair and could be opened in the actual LihatDulu viewer. Keep the early seven-take panels, matched local overnight matrix, and cloud result details in Lampiran C. Distinguish the authored cabinet from the real chair study. The real mesh shows overall recognizable form. Fine wear assessment, scale accuracy, consistent quality across items, and generalization to new footage have their own evidence requirements. In product prose, describe what buyers do in the authored demonstration and give the measured real result once, in its own exhibit.

## Product, scan, and deployment evidence

### Buyer-facing product

LihatDulu's current authored catalog has ten active examples, two marked coming soon, and four authored 3D/room assets. Vite, JavaScript, Three.js, CSS, SparkJS and a Node scan server remain the implementation stack. The marketplace flow includes a buyer/seller gateway, catalog search, categories, URL-backed filters, sorting, saved items, comparison, offers, cart, and a simulated checkout review. The buyer gets product photos, condition notes, and seller-provided dimensions. Supported authored assets have 3D inspection and room-placement controls. The prepared seller walkthrough uses the 18-second Sora example. Cart, offers and checkout illustrate local behaviors. Real payment, live seller contact, orders, stock/ownership, and a persistent commercial marketplace remain unverified by the prototype.

The redesign uses a white canvas, green selection/actions, and legible catalog metadata. The current source exposes the filter rail around 900 px, and mobile has a labeled filter control with categories and item prices apparent. Browser checks cover navigation, filter cancel/reload persistence, collections, price consistency, condition details, narrow viewports, fixed surfaces, and the prepared seller flow. Use real captured screenshots of current routes for proposal exhibits. The design-pilot screenshots document the earlier design comparison and can be used for history, not as proof of a later branch's exact presentation. [Marketplace brief and tests](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/blob/8b404df/docs/handoff/workstreams/marketplace.md) · [Design comparison](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/tree/8b404df/docs/handoff/design)

### Original real chair study on the local worker

On `main`, `/jual/pindai` implements camera capture and upload. The Node server serves the site and scan API, with one disk-backed worker using FFmpeg, COLMAP 4.2.1, Brush 0.3.0 and SPZ export. `/pindai/:id` opens a distinct scan preview linked to its source stills and metadata. Study replays came from existing phone camera originals processed on the Helios/RTX 4060 setup. Keep its phone-to-laptop HTTPS acceptance separate from the worker evidence.

Seven eligible clips of **one chair** yielded shareable models. One 3.33 s false start was excluded, and five compressed WhatsApp copies were set aside. Extracted-frame registration was 100% within those eligible runs. Worker time was ~244–333 s, with SPZ artifacts ~0.34–0.68 MiB. These figures measure actual worker completion and artifact size in a bounded setup. Local steps, hardware, and per-take details are retained in the archive and pinned branch. Evidence refers to seven recordings of a chair, not seven independently tested products.

The early automated label “recognizable chair” was a pixel-coverage/clipping heuristic. The saved images show haze, off-center fragments and distortion, confirmed by reviewer visual inspection in this chat. The initial seven views therefore establish renderability, not a usable product inspection. The reported 1280 “long edge” in some older notes is also unreliable for the portrait clips. The actual worker preset fixed width and the published portrait stills measure 1280 × 2276; see the archive for that audit. [Study comparison](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/blob/cbf69f0/var/spike/comparison.md) · [Baseline audit](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/blob/8b404df/overnight-scan/AUDIT.md) · [Published comparison sheet](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/blob/8b404df/overnight-scan/evidence/published-views.jpg)

### Completed local overnight run

Run `20261122-153241` is now published as a curated outcome, superseding the “run not yet verified” part of the previous edition. Its strongest result is a **diagnostic** with an opt-in profile and real, independently reproducible viewer behavior. The profile was not promoted. Read [MORNING.md](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/blob/f5767f6/var/overnight/20261122-153241/MORNING.md), [HANDOFF.md](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/blob/f5767f6/var/overnight/20261122-153241/HANDOFF.md), the ledger and [matched comparison](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/blob/f5767f6/var/overnight/20261122-153241/comparisons/frozen-config-sheet.jpg).

- The implementation corrected a real geometry bug: the viewer previously subtracted the original center while also rotating the splat. It now subtracts the rotated center, frames on bounded subject data, and offers a stable orbit, deterministic reset, and pose retention on resize. A QA harness exercises named views, mouse and touch, resize, route disposal/reentry and reload.
- The `SCAN_PROFILE=chair` profile estimates the object center by intersecting aligned camera axes and removes most of the room using a sphere. It uses `clamp(0.9 × r90, 1.8, 2.7)`, with r90 measured on the opaque mass about its median, and uses 6,000 training steps. The default pipeline is separate and retains its original behavior. Commits `6363101`, `24c0e04`, `5f782ae`, `c51a704`, `58148a1`, `5e28473`, and `d001013` cover the viewer, QA, opt-in cleanup, hardening, and records.
- Golden (25.35 s, 76/76 registered) has a readable front-quarter pose, streaks across the arms in another view, and haze or thin detail elsewhere. The best manually cropped published SPZ is 124,705 bytes and 5,958 splats. The calibrated rule reproduced that result closely at 6,096 splats. The source coverage was an incomplete arc, so the registration count cannot substitute for rear/underside evidence.
- Repeat (20.13 s, 60/60 registered) ran completely through the real opt-in worker in about 420 s, produced 4,993 splats and ~0.10 MiB, and still smears around the lower/rear portion. The 10.03 s stress take failed visually even after cleanup. Only Repeat reran through the full opt-in pipeline in that continuation. Golden reused preserved alignment.
- Reported Windows runs include 226/226 unit, 26/26 marketplace browser, 15/15 simulated scan, 4/4 orbit and 17/17 lifecycle checks. These records distinguish actual reconstruction, software-rendered playback, simulated camera, and physical-phone behavior. None of this overnight run demonstrated a physical phone capturing in the live site and reopening its own result over HTTPS.

### Cloud scan branch and latest experiments

The KIRI branch keeps the marketplace and the authored Sora demonstration. It replaces the local reconstruction worker and guided in-browser recorder **within that branch** with a file-upload flow. Choose an existing MP4, preview before sending, explicitly approve cloud processing, submit, then see upload/queue/processing/download status and open the stored result in the LihatDulu viewer. Reload restores the same job. Browser and server do not need local COLMAP, Brush or CUDA. Stills-based modes use FFmpeg to sample the original video. The app keeps the provider key server-side and can run a clearly labeled mock. Jobs are disk-backed, provider serials tie to application jobs, in-flight/successful duplicate uploads are deduped, and completed mesh and splat files have distinct service and viewer paths. Accounts, ownership, listing publication from a scan, durable multi-user operations, and the physical-browser capture flow remain outside the proven KIRI scope.

Important implementation checkpoints in the 11-commit KIRI branch are `457da34` initial upload prototype, `c35a97c` live response/upload handling and backdrop removal, photo-set submission and capture-derived provenance in `efdca96`/`72bbe9d`/`fbced73`, `052bca8` experimental splat crop, `9eb84bf` recorded quality matrix, `16e2c5f` first-class featureless GLB, `7b2789c` lossless evidence captures, and `9c1c73b` the distinct Photo Scan mode, quality metadata, and revised mesh lighting.

| KIRI evidence | Observed outcome and scope |
|---|---|
| First real video submit | The Golden clip was about 25.3 s, 61 MB and portrait. One documented job reached ready in 2,274 s (37 min 54 s), including about 282 s for the server-to-provider upload and ~29 minutes of queue/processing. Published notes report 9/9 checks of transport, state, viewer and reload, including a 390 px browser viewport. The job initially showed a synthetic backdrop shell. Removing 50,000 background splats exposed a hollow, unusable reconstruction. This was transport/rendering evidence, with a failed output-quality verdict. |
| Overnight KIRI matrix, 22–23 November | Seven of eight historical authorized credits were spent. The first job in the matrix became orphaned when the serial was lost. The other video splats and the two 101-still splat variants were cluttered room exports or melted object shapes. Video runs were measured at ~24–27 min and stills splats at ~13–21 min on that setup. Splat cropping could not rescue these captures because the camera axes estimate missed the subject. The KIRI run notes report one remaining credit at that time. That is a historical tally, not a fresh balance or permission for a paid run. |
| Featureless mesh, the strongest real result | A set of **101 stills sampled from the Golden clip** went to the featureless-object mesh route. The notes record ~36 min for that job stage and the LihatDulu screenshot reports **39 min 56 s** total. The GLB is 89,582,948 bytes (~85 MiB), with 815,094 triangles. Actual in-app screenshots show seat, arms, backrest and some base/casters; rear/top show texture bleed and room geometry. The shipped viewer makes the shell double-sided and chooses a three-quarter opening pose. The reported 2.8 s desktop and 2.4 s at 390 × 844 were the local real-artifact browser harness reaching ready. They do not cover cloud processing or represent handset/network benchmarks. |
| Latest `Photo Scan` code | Four modes are now `video` (default video splat), `splat` (stills to splats), `photo` (Photo Scan textured mesh), and `mesh` (the separate featureless-object GLB profile). Older notes used `photo` for the still-to-splat path. The latest commit gives the new Photo Scan route model/texture quality and smoothing options, records them in provenance, handles GLB results, and updates mesh lighting. The previously published recognizable chair used `mesh`. The audit found implementation, tests, and mock behavior for the new work, with live Photo Scan quality still unverified. |

The KIRI docs, local run journal, and viewer caption serve different timing scopes. Quote the model as a one-chair proof with the total elapsed time visible in the result screenshot, using rounded language if needed. Timing, file size, visual quality, and scan acceptance on real hardware should each be reported against their own boundary. Keep raw clips and any credentials in the private local environment. The public repo only has selected visuals for the newest KIRI run.

### Deployment and viewer delivery

`dist/` is tracked. Its last committed update on `main` and the overnight code branch is the imported baseline. A fresh build replaces several output files, including the route HTML setup, so the development/server test paths and the commit's `dist` are separate deliverables. A previously reproduced stale bundle showed the missing state at `/pindai/<job>`. A fresh local build produced the scan routes. The KIRI branch has a later committed interim bundle at `c35a97c`, still earlier than the final mesh/Photo Scan source. The Node service serves local scan APIs and the SPA. A static deployment needs explicit deep-link routing for browser refreshes and a compatible route to the intended backend if a live scan is part of the demo.

The advertised [public homepage](https://lihatdulu-sooty.vercel.app/) returned HTTP 200 and served a built JS filename matching the fresh `main` root bundle. Direct HTTP requests to `/beli`, `/jual`, and `/jual/pindai` returned 404 at audit time. The audit also saw a 404 on a probe URL for a nonexistent scan job. It did not exercise an authorized live job on that public URL. The main site's in-app navigation and freshly built local browser tests are separate from those public direct-route checks. Verify the actual links and device/network setup that judges will use before claiming the real online capture result works. A saved real capture can be a prepared artifact for a rehearsal and should be described as such.

## Fresh verification and maturity assessment

The following is this chat's independent Linux audit. It used fresh local builds from the published refs, Node 24.22.3, and tests in the isolated worktrees. It did not use a real camera, contact KIRI with a new live job, download private result media, or modify the published repository. The initial offline dependency install lacked a cached package; a normal dependency install then succeeded and all three code branches built. The bundles retained their chunk-size advisories.

| Ref or surface | Verified in this audit |
|---|---|
| `main` at `8b404df` | Fresh build, **196/197 unit**, **26/26 browser**. The lone unit failure is the existing Windows path assertion in `tests/scan-pipeline.test.js` under Linux. Earlier repo Windows runs recorded 197/197. |
| `work/scan-quality-overnight-20261122-153241` at `d001013` | Fresh build, **225/226 unit** with the same path-format failure, and **29/29** focused cleanup and viewer tests when the temporary directory was placed in the workspace. The orbit and lifecycle totals above come from the published Windows run. |
| `feat/kiri-scan` at `9c1c73b` | Fresh build, **253/253 unit**, **118/118** targeted KIRI/scan tests (overlaps the unit suite), and **20/20 KIRI mock browser checks**. Checks included consent gating, local preview, one submit/resume, recovery, GLB routing, rotation, reload and mobile-width layout. This was a mock-provider run. |
| Published visuals | Compared the seven original SPZ previews, the matched Golden/Repeat overnight views, the KIRI four-angle sheet and the actual KIRI result-page screenshot, plus fresh desktop/mobile marketplace screenshots. |
| Public site and GitHub | Checked the homepage and deep-route response behavior; checked seven refs, 58 commits reachable across them, and all five PR states. No GitHub Releases or tags observed at cutoff. The real cloud jobs/credits and absent physical-device results stayed outside independent test scope. |

**Maturity judgment from the audit.** The buyer UI is a late-stage interactive prototype. It is capable of a helpful judge demonstration and is causally connected to the condition/fit problem, with prepared inventory and simulated commerce. Real scanning has a credible integration proof and now a visible 3D object, with quality, repeatability, latency and size work left before it is a dependable listing tool. Demo delivery is a release problem as well as a scan problem. The judge-facing link, reload, preview and device should be checked together. The venture is at exploratory customer-evidence stage. The gap is evidence about which listing uncertainties decide purchases, what work sellers currently do, and which segment values the offer enough to pay or return. Production means real data, seller ownership, fulfillment, checkout and operational controls, all beyond the current demonstration evidence.

**Suggested order for the next decision, subject to user direction.** Lock the intended judging URLs and test on the physical device with the saved KIRI result first. Use Sora to show the coherent buyer journey and the real chair footage-to-mesh as a separate technical proof. If more scan work is authorized, compare a second, deliberately captured piece or take and measure minimum detail, orbit usability and load time. Use the six-question interviews to ground the problem and target market while the finance teammate chooses the funding structure. At pricing time, compare seller effort and retakes with the revenue event. Avoid spending time adding more frameworks or catalogue breadth before those gaps are addressed.

## Read this first

LihatDulu is an Indonesian secondhand furniture marketplace concept for a business-plan competition. The buyer experience helps people choose a piece by condition, price, location, and fit. The seller experience now has both the authored Sora example and an implemented real scan route. The prototype has demonstrated local reconstruction from original phone footage. The seven saved chair scans still fail the initial visual inspection standard, and the intended phone → HTTPS → Helios worker → same-phone preview flow is open.

Read this November update first. It supersedes the October pack's older claims about the live code, deployment layout, lack of camera/backend, and any earlier proposal decisions that differ from the November record. The **complete original 16 October V8.0 pack** follows this update as a byte-for-byte archive, including its V5.1, V5, V4 and V3 history. Its own opening and continuation prompts belong to that edition. The repository version remains 8.0.0. The date identifies this context edition.

**Authority:** latest explicit owner decision → actual checked source and selected evidence → applicable dated verification → this November handoff → October V8 and earlier historical material. A newer local session may have unpublished changes. Check Git branches, `var/overnight/LATEST.txt`, and the run ledger before asserting a newer implementation or repeating work. ## Current product and implementation

- **Product story.** Buyers search and filter secondhand furniture, then check dimensions, condition and the seller before making a decision. For selected authored products they can rotate the model, inspect notes and see how it fits in a room. Sellers can explore the prepared evidence journey or try real capture and review the generated buyer preview.
- **Catalogue.** Ten active authored examples and two coming soon. Four authored GLBs support model/room behavior. Listing IDs, price and policy, evidence eligibility and known visual assets remain defined by the current source. The original V8 catalogue and geometry tables below are a reference, with changes governed by the current checkout.
- **Marketplace behavior.** Buyer search, categories, URL-backed filters, stable sort, comparison, saved items, offers, cart, checkout rehearsal and review are part of the prototype. Buyer records and checkout outcomes are local demos. The marketplace has no live payments, real seller contact or real commerce workflow. Visible state distinctions matter for a judge-facing demo.
- **Visual system.** Platform white canvas, charcoal text around `#1b2420`, neutral media wells, and restraint around green `#176044` for actions and selection. Desktop catalogue filter rail begins at 900 px, with responsive mobile filter sheet and visible categories. Remove decorative eyebrow copy where it repeats the surrounding screen. Keep information needed for actions and meaningful state. The October sage canvas describes the prior product design and is useful as historical provenance, including its influence on the proposal artwork.
- **Demo boundaries.** Sora's 18-second prepared walkthrough covers six named capture areas. Its authored GLB and motion/room behavior are independent of real video-to-SPZ reconstruction. Do not substitute Sora assets for a new scan, imply ownership or verification, or make new scans into catalogue listings without a separate product decision. Source stills, measured dimensions supplied by the user, and processed splats are different kinds of evidence.

### Runtime and build

The checkout is plain JS, CSS, Vite, Three.js and SparkJS, with Plus Jakarta Sans and the pinned dependencies in `package-lock.json`. The checked project includes Vite 8.3.2, Three.js 0.186.1, Spark 2.4.0 and Playwright 1.63.0. The root project layout replaces the older ZIP's nested `app/` layout for this checkout. The audited checkout has no root Vercel config. The root README retains an outdated claim about no backend. The actual scan implementation and dated handoff settle that discrepancy.

```sh
npm ci
npm run test:unit
npm run build
npm run test:browser
node scripts/verify-scan.mjs
```

Use Node 24.x for the tested setup. Build in an isolated worktree if preserving tracked `dist/`. Building changes the prerendered route HTML and hashed assets. The tracked `dist/` came from the imported baseline, so serve a fresh build for any scan QA or rehearsal. `npm run dev` runs Vite on loopback and proxies `/api` to port 8787. `npm run scan:api` serves only the API, `npm run scan:serve` serves the current build and scan API, and `npm run scan:fake` supplies a fixture for QA. Treat an actual competition deployment as a separate action. Rebuild and inspect route handling before presenting or packaging it.

## Real scan pipeline and machine

| Item | Dated implementation or observation |
|---|---|
| Hardware | Acer Predator Helios Neo 16, PHN16-71, 13th-gen i7, 32 GB RAM. Recorded lab environment Windows 11 Home Single Language, RTX 4060 Laptop GPU with 8,188 MiB and driver 581.80, Node 24.19.0. Verify current versions locally. |
| Tools | FFmpeg/ffprobe 8.1.2-full_build, COLMAP 4.2.1 CUDA (bd1fcf6), Brush CLI 0.3.0, PlayCanvas splat-transform 3.12.0 (5d5be2b). COLMAP is usually `var/tools/colmap/bin/colmap.exe`; Brush is `var/tools/brush/brush_app.exe`. Provide absolute `SCAN_COLMAP` and `SCAN_BRUSH` overrides. |
| Input | Rear camera preferred, video only, explicit tap, secure browser context, MP4 or WebM depending on recorder support. In-app 8–45 s and server tolerance 7–50 s. Upload limit 104,857,600 bytes. Manual title/dimension inputs and optional condition note are job metadata. |
| Extraction | FFmpeg, nominal base 3 fps, adaptive up to 8 to meet 60 frames, VFR retry if actual extracted count misses the floor. Current filter `fps=…,scale=1280:-2` fixes width. Measure actual display rotation and written frame size. |
| Alignment | One OPENCV camera, sequential matches with overlap 10 and loop detection off in the audited preset. Examine all numeric sparse sub-models and select greatest registration. Worker gate 20 registered and 30% of extracted, plus 60 total extracted. The historical Golden experiment had a stricter 60% registration gate. Both are technical gates. |
| Training and export | Brush 3,000 steps in the audited default, max requested 2.0M splats. Produce PLY and convert with splat-transform `--filter-nan`, delivering SPZ and source stills. Result cap 60 MiB and 2.5M splats, with a decimation branch. |
| Service | One disk-backed Node worker, queued jobs, same-origin UI and API. Stages `queued → validating → extracting → aligning → reconstructing → finalizing → ready`, plus failures. Job IDs act as capabilities. Default queue limit 3 and retention 10 finished jobs. Result stays in the local preview and does not become a listing. |
| Storage | `SCAN_ROOT`, `SCAN_DIST`, `SCAN_PORT`, and `SCAN_KEEP` can isolate a run. The worker deletes its temporary aligned dataset after success. The server prunes terminal jobs on start and after processing. Copy reusable alignment into an isolated run before cleanup and protect accepted outputs. |

The scan page currently advances guidance on a timer. It tells sellers to orbit, then suggests open drawers and wear close-ups during that same take. The study's recommended geometry pass is a slow, continuous orbit at two heights, preferably 15–25 seconds, clear overlap, full stationary object in frame, enough side light for dark material, and no strong backlight. Start with the existing footage for any overnight experiments. Test full-orbit coverage and source views before making guidance claims. Treat recording detail close-ups as a separate future input to the geometry pass.

Official CLI details, old smoke test, known failures and benchmark inputs are in the [scan-toolchain notes](https://github.com/Fafauzif/Lihatdulu/blob/a26d952/docs/handoff/scan-toolchain.md). The prepared Sora clip registered only 2/54 in that earlier smoke. The public South Building example produced a renderable SPZ, which established tool wiring. The chair originals are the real furniture evidence.

## Chair study and evidence

The 22 November lab study screened eight uncompressed camera originals. Seven eligible recordings made ready SPZ results, and all seven registered every extracted frame. The 3.33-second false start was excluded. Five compressed WhatsApp copies were archived outside the tested set. “Golden” and “Repeat” are study roles. Golden means the reference take under the technical study gate. It does not imply an approved visual model.

| Role | Original clip | Duration | Registered | Worker time | Splats | SPZ MiB |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Golden / tuning | `VID_20261121_184227976.mp4` | 25.35 s | 76/76 | 291.281 s | 27,447 | 0.52 |
| Repeat / confirmation | `VID_20261121_184349140.mp4` | 20.13 s | 60/60 | 289.718 s | 25,181 | 0.47 |
| Short stress take, supplied screenshot | `VID_20261121_184212135.mp4` | 10.03 s | 60/60 | 332.873 s | 34,342 | 0.68 |
| Variant | `VID_20261121_184320272.mp4` | 10.21 s | 60/60 | 244.142 s | 25,811 | 0.50 |
| Variant | `VID_20261121_184453628.mp4` | 14.59 s | 72/72 | 259.377 s | 27,466 | 0.51 |
| Variant / extraction retry | `VID_20261121_184626753.mp4` | 15.00 s | 74/74 | 269.539 s | 33,284 | 0.62 |
| Variant / 4K encoded source | `VID_20261121_184714115.mp4` | 14.62 s | 71/71 | 245.222 s | 18,461 | 0.34 |

The study fixed three operational issues while keeping the technical thresholds. The batch job root is resolved before worker execution. The worker now chooses the largest registered numeric COLMAP sub-model, which recovered the Repeat run from a misleading `sparse/0` with only two registered views. The capped extraction retry addressed the 15-second VFR take, where 4 fps yielded 59 frames and the 5 fps re-extract yielded 74. The table's extraction time for that one clip includes both passes. Full raw benchmark rows and comparison notes are on the evidence branch. The 4K source yielded the smallest current artifact, which does not establish that 4K sharpness improved or harmed the result. Both 1080p and 4K encoded inputs produced the same dimensions in the provided stills.
